[SOURCE:ALBRECHT_ENTRATI]
============================================================================================================================================

[CASE:2_MELLO]
[PRIMARY_Q:CAUSAL LEGIBILITY]
2 Mello [real -- producer/composer, USA; Memories of
  Tokyo-To, MILLION SUNDAY series and others]
  Type: Primary [S:3]
  Proof for: ART COHERENCE TYPE [SOMATIC -- geometric
    variety, memory-sourced; ENCOUNTER-DEPENDENT]; ART
    COHERENCE TYPE [RELATIONAL variety; ENCOUNTER-DEPENDENT].
    Structural address [object-readable]: compositional
    properties [rhythmic feel, timbral character, harmonic
    approach] sourced from Jet Set Radio and similar game
    music -- structural properties that carry pre-loaded
    geometry for instruments with matched developmental
    exposure. Whether geometric encounter occurs depends
    on whether instrument formed during the relevant
    period [high formation-window exposure to that game
    music]. An instrument without that exposure reads the
    same structural properties without the pre-loaded
    geometry -- a genuine mismatch, not a lesser encounter.
    RELATIONAL variety: structural address routes
    recognition toward the listener's own past state
    rather than toward the performer [design choice,
    object-readable in the work's framing]. Whether
    recognition encounter occurs is instrument-dependent.
  Extraction mechanisms active: none.
  Cross-ref: ART COHERENCE TYPE [SOMATIC MUSIC -- geometric
    type, memory-sourced]; ART COHERENCE TYPE [RELATIONAL
    variety]; [REF:FUTURE_FUNK_CLUSTER] [geometric type
    comparison -- sample-sourced vs memory-sourced].

[SOURCE:WARFRAME]
============================================================================================================================================
Warframe [Digital Extremes -- fiction S2; hub formed 2026-11-05]
 Type: Primary source [Tier 1 -- in-game quest / codex / Leverian gate]
 Hub: SOURCE cluster [7 live CASEs across 3 arcs + 4 mechanism lanes]
 **Dense-read protocol:** Three story scales [Orokin pre-history E0-E2 /
 Void War + Narmer E3-E6 / Operator-Drifter gate E7-E9]; each CASE promotes
 from a Tier 1 primary [quest text, Leverian, codex] -- desk recap and wiki
 are orientation only. Update = Tier 1 quest pass then arc delta OR one
 mechanism body.
 Sibling sources [tier map -- not separate kit SOURCE bodies]:
 Tier 2: wiki / orokinarchives transcript [T3 until in-game confirmed]
 Tier 3: official shorts [Styanax Ascension Day, PlayWarframe/Passion Paris
 2022-08-26]; desk YT recap [orientation only]
 Rule: load-bearing mechanism pins require Tier 1 primary; T2-T3 illustrate
 until H1 closes.

[CASE:ZUKO]
[PRIMARY_Q:GENUINE AXIS]
Zuko [Avatar: The Last Airbender]
  Type: Primary [S:2]
  Arc: Evil -> Mixed -> Close True [AGC escape arc -- cleanest
    in library]
  Phase boundaries:
    Evil: series start -> Ba Sing Se betrayal [end Book 2]
    Mixed: Book 3 pre-Iroh reconciliation
    Close True: Iroh reconciliation -> series end

  FOUNDATION:
    GENUINE AXIS: Evil = axis imposed [Ozai approval].
      Mixed = imposed axis fracturing; no replacement.
      Close True = genuine axis fires [beach house -> throne
        room arc]. "I've been a better person since joining
        the Avatar."
    SUBSTRATE REGENERATION: Evil = Iroh actively regenerates
      Zuko's substrate; Zuko rejects it.
      Mixed = SUBSTRATE REGENERATION critically depleted
        [Book 3 illness = visible failure].
      Close True = Iroh as substrate source restored.

  LEVEL 1: GENERATIVE CYCLE: Evil = cycle running on extraction
    -- capturing Avatar = taking coherence to feed Ozai-approval
    loop. Close True = firing on genuine output [protection,
    training]. SURPLUS PRODUCTION: Evil = negative. Close True
    = positive. CORRECTION CAPACITY: Evil = captured by Ozai.
    Close True = free and aimed correctly.

  LEVEL 2: MEASUREMENT INTEGRITY: Evil = measured against
    "does this restore Ozai's approval." Close True = rebuilt
    against actual outputs. CAUSAL LEGIBILITY: Evil = chain
    obscured by AGC. Close True = fully legible. "My father
    burned me" = causal statement, not shame frame.

  LEVEL 3: TRANSMISSION SELECTIVITY: Close True = transmits
    redirected firebending, political shift. GAP: escape method
    not transmitted. SUCCESSOR AUTONOMY: strongest C3 quality.
    Explicitly refuses to impose axis on Aang [lightning scene].

  WHY CLOSE TRUE NOT TRUE:
    1. TRANSMISSION SELECTIVITY package incomplete -- AGC escape
       not transmitted as correctable method.
    2. L3.5 not tested post-arc.
    3. SUBSTRATE REGENERATION still Iroh-dependent.

  AGC MECHANISM [why arc is clean]:
    Installation event: Ozai's Agni Kai [pre-examination].
    Break requirement: near-total substrate collapse required.
    Iroh's function: substrate maintenance during collapse window.
    Cleanest AGC case in library [fictional].

  Extraction mechanisms active [received]: Axis Gestation Capture [SUCCESSOR
    AUTONOMY], Formation Capture [SUCCESSOR AUTONOMY], Exit Cost
    Construction [GENERATIVE CYCLE], Calibration Strip
    [TRANSMISSION SELECTIVITY].

    unconditional presence, warmth without correction pressure,
    consistency. Intellectual challenge from Aang/Katara
    produced no recovery. Same information, different axis.

[CASE:GUTS]
[PRIMARY_Q:GENUINE AXIS]
Guts [Berserk]
  Type: Primary [S:2]
  Proof for: Mixed individual-scale [stable]; internal tension
    not external extraction mechanism. Mixed-stable; directionality
    window-dependent. Three-axis: center, variable by window,
    front to mid.
  Extraction mechanisms active: none [internal tension].

  CHAPTER 384 UPDATE [v22 -- "no self" reveal; T9 RESOLUTION
  candidate]:

  FUNCTIONAL READ [in-universe structure]:
    Guts has run for the entire series on RAGE as a captured
      drive [Griffith's betrayal as permanent external trigger]
      layered over GENUINE AXIS [the actual underlying desire:
      to live, to protect Casca, to find meaning past mere
      survival]. The rage was functional -- it produced power
      and forward motion -- but extractive: the Berserker
      Armor converts health and sanity directly into power,
      the textbook ANTI-COHERENCE COST ASYMMETRY signature
      [extraction works short-term; cost compounds with use].
    Chapter 384's "no self" reveal [Guts born half-astral, from
      the same cosmic substrate as the godhand, and confronting
      his full origin via Flora's fire-spirit vision] reframes
      the upcoming power-up not as more force layered onto the
      same rage-engine, but as the removal of the parasitic
      rage-structure entirely. This is GENUINE AXIS reaching
      actual resolution rather than perpetual costly management
      -- T9 RESOLUTION: the axis was always there underneath
      the captured drive; the chapter is the integration event,
      not a new power source.
    Griffith's established power [reality bending to his desire;
      Dragon Slayer being one of very few things that can land
      damage on a godhand, yet nothing touches Griffith himself]
      is read here as FORMATION CAPTURE operating at narrative-
      structural scale within the fiction: Griffith's desire
      reshapes events because the story's own causality serves
      his arc -- in-fiction, the routing boundary and the
      measurement surface are the same entity, his will. Guts
      achieving "no self" and being framed as someone who can
      "exist outside the author's pen" is GENUINE AXIS reaching
      a state the captured system's rules cannot process --
      not winning within Griffith's rules by being stronger,
      but exiting the frame those rules operate inside. Same
      structural shape as [CASE:WHEEL_OF_TIME]'s resolution-by-
      exiting-the-corrupted-pattern rather than fighting it on
      its own terms.
    CONNECTION TO [CASE:IDEA_OF_EVIL]: Guts's origin [condensed
      from the astral world's cosmic ocean, the same substrate
      that produces the godhand and is adjacent to the Idea of
      Evil's domain] ties his GENUINE AXIS resolution directly
      to the closed-loop topology IDEA_OF_EVIL represents.
      Where the Idea of Evil is a system generated by and
      consuming the population that produces it [T3 unreachable
      as a topology property -- see IDEA_OF_EVIL entry], Guts
      achieving "no self" is the individual-scale exit from
      that same closed loop: ego-driven wish-making is what
      feeds the Idea of Evil; "no self" is structurally the
      one stance that cannot be converted into more fuel for
      it. Read together, Guts's arc is the individual-scale
      proof-of-concept for escaping the civilizational-scale
      trap IDEA_OF_EVIL names.

  META READ [production/authorial layer -- distinct from the
  in-universe read above]:
    The chapter's in-story theme [a character achieving
      independence from authorial/causal control within the
      fiction] lands at the exact moment its real-world
      production circumstances mirror that theme closely: Kouji
      Mori, taking over the story after Kentaro Miura's death,
      executing a reveal Miura reportedly planned roughly 20
      years prior, reconstructed through repeated conversation
      with Miura's longtime editor rather than from Miura's
      direct hand. This is TRANSMISSION SELECTIVITY and
      SUCCESSOR AUTONOMY playing out in reality at the same
      moment the fiction depicts a protagonist breaking free of
      its author's narrative control.
    Two readings, not resolved here: [a] striking coincidence --
      the thematic content of a 20-year-old planned reveal
      happened to land at a moment when its execution required
      exactly the kind of successor-reconstructs-original-intent
      process the chapter's content also describes; or [b]
      deliberate choice -- Mori folding the real succession
      story into how he chose to execute the reveal, aware of
      the parallel.
    HONEST LIMIT: this meta-layer claim rests on a single
      secondary source [a YouTube video summarizing and
      interpreting the chapter, including a quoted statement
      attributed to Mori about the reveal's 20-year planning
      and reconstruction process via Miura's editor]. Not
      independently verified against a primary source. Treat
      the functional read above as the more confidently
      supportable claim; treat the meta read as a flagged,
      lower-confidence interpretive layer pending verification.
    Cross-ref: SUCCESSOR AUTONOMY [Section One -- the quality
      this meta-layer is actually about]; TRANSMISSION
      SELECTIVITY [what determines how much of Miura's original
      intent survives reconstruction through an intermediary].

  Cross-ref: [REF:GUTS]; [CASE:GRIFFITH] [primary antagonist;
    FORMATION CAPTURE at narrative-structural scale]; [CASE:
    IDEA_OF_EVIL] [closed-loop topology Guts's origin and
    resolution connect to directly]; T9 [RESOLUTION -- axis
    integration rather than perpetual management]; GENUINE
    AXIS [Section One -- primary case for resolution after
    long captured-drive management]; ANTI-COHERENCE COST
    ASYMMETRY [Berserker Armor as the clean extraction-cost
    signature this resolution removes]; SUCCESSOR AUTONOMY
    [meta read -- real-world production parallel].

[CASE:DEATH_PARADE]
[PRIMARY_Q: MEASUREMENT INTEGRITY]
Death Parade [Yuzuru Tachikawa / Madhouse -- anime, 2015]
  Type: Primary [S:2 -- fictional; framework-consistent;
    multiple quality proofs; S2 cap applies]
  Proof for: MEASUREMENT INTEGRITY [primary -- the arbiter
    system is a correction architecture built on a measurement
    instrument that cannot read what it is measuring. Arbiters
    have never lived or died -- they are structurally outside
    the substrate they are judging. The instrument cannot
    access the full weight of human experience from inside
    its own formation. The death games produce behavior under
    fabricated mortal crisis with erased memory; the arbiters
    read this as revelation of true nature. But behavior under
    extreme artificial pressure is not a causal record of the
    soul's actual moral history. The MI failure is not
    incidental -- it is the design. The system was built by
    entities too detached from humanity to build anything that
    could read humanity accurately, and they neither know nor
    care when they get it wrong].
  Proof for: CAUSAL LEGIBILITY [behavior under artificial
    mortal threat misread as moral record. The games are
    designed to extract darkness. They extract darkness-under-
    pressure, which is not the same thing. A person who
    becomes violent when told their life is on the line is
    not the same as a person who was violent across their
    actual life. The instrument reads forced crisis as causal
    trace. CL failure: outputs not traceable to the actions
    that determined the soul's actual trajectory].
  Proof for: CC REQUIRES GENUINE AXIS [Decim arc -- arbiter
    without genuine axis cannot correct. Decim is a dummy:
    built, not lived. His axis is the rules of the system
    that formed him. Chiyuki [black-haired woman] is the
    correction mechanism inserted from outside -- the thing
    that makes Decim's instrument revisable. She is what
    genuine axis contact looks like from outside the system.
    Without her: the instrument runs its attractor forever.
    With her: the instrument can begin examining whether the
    attractor produces what the system claims to value].
  Proof for: DIAGNOSTIC COLLAPSE [the correct judgment in
    most episodes would require C1 substrate reading that
    the arbiters cannot perform. They restore C2 [judgment
    surface] without C1 audit. The result: souls with
    recoverable substrate sent to the void, which is
    permanent removal from the generative cycle. C2
    restoration without C1 audit produces collapse not
    recovery -- confirmed in every episode where a
    defensible soul gets voided because the measurement
    instrument read crisis behavior as true nature].
  Proof for: AXIS PROTECTION AS CC [Nona vs Oculus --
    Nona is attempting to correct the system from inside.
    Oculus owns the correction architecture and owns the
    rule [no emotions in arbiters] that makes the system
    unable to correct. The system's CC is owned by the
    node that benefits from the system running uncorrected.
    Four-condition test fails at the top: no correction
    pathway that can fire against Oculus independently.
    Nona's workaround [smuggling Chiyuki in] is the only
    available correction path -- which is why it requires
    smuggling].

  STRUCTURAL ARC [Decim and Chiyuki]:
    Decim starts as a pure instrument -- accurate within
    the limits of his formation, unable to examine those
    limits. Chiyuki is the external axis contact that
    makes examination possible. The arc is not "Decim
    learns to feel" -- it is "Decim's instrument gains
    the capacity to read what it could not read before."
    The cost: the system reads this as corruption. An
    arbiter who can feel is an arbiter whose judgment
    might deviate from the institutional axis. The
    institution's interest is in arbiters who cannot
    examine their own formation.
    Chiyuki's final judgment [Decim chooses reincarnation
    over void for someone who attempted suicide] is the
    case where the corrected instrument produces a
    different output than the uncorrected one would have.
    The correction worked. The system did not sanction it.

  VOID AS EXTRACTION MECHANISM:
    The void is permanent removal from the generative
    cycle. Not punishment -- permanent substrate
    destruction at eschatological scale. The system's
    error rate produces void sentences on souls that
    don't warrant it. The damage is not recoverable.
    This is the DIAGNOSTIC COLLAPSE finding at maximum
    resolution: the measurement instrument fails, the
    correction fires based on the failed reading, and
    the correction is irreversible. No C1 audit possible
    after the void sentence executes.

  Extraction mechanisms active: Calibration Strip
    [arbiters set "behavior under mortal crisis" as
    the reference standard for "true nature" -- the
    calibration standard is wrong by design]; Formation
    Capture [arbiters formed without human experience;
    the thing they cannot read is the thing they were
    never given access to during formation].
  T3: S2 cap. Fictional case; framework-consistent.
    Do not use for primary theory confirmation.
  Cross-ref: MEASUREMENT INTEGRITY [primary failure];
    CAUSAL LEGIBILITY [crisis behavior ≠ causal record];
    CC REQUIRES GENUINE AXIS [Decim arc]; DIAGNOSTIC
    COLLAPSE [void sentence on misread soul]; AXIS
    PROTECTION AS CC [Oculus / Nona]; FORMATION CAPTURE
    [arbiter formation without substrate access];
    [CASE:ERGO_PROXY] [similar structure -- CC
    monopolized at theological scale then abandoned;
    instruments operating with concealed axis];
    [CASE:GLASNOST] [Diagnostic Collapse primary case --
    C2 restoration without C1 audit].

[CASE:DISCO_ELYSIUM]
[PRIMARY_Q:GENUINE AXIS]
Disco Elysium [ZA/UM -- 2019; RPG]
  Type: Primary [S:2; fictional; multiple quality proofs;
    S2 cap applies]

  THE SKILL SYSTEM AS QUALITY STACK EXTERNALIZED:
    24 skills are not stats -- they are named sub-instruments
    within the composite instrument [Harry Du Bois], each
    with distinct voice, distinct failure modes, distinct
    capture vulnerabilities. The framework's quality stack
    rendered as characters who argue with each other.
    VOLITION [CYCLE STABILITY]: keeps Harry functional when
      everything else is degrading. The quality that enables
      the other qualities to fire. Without Volition: Harry
      cannot maintain the generative cycle; substance use
      wins; the instrument collapses. With Volition: the
      instrument can hold the weight of what it discovers.
    INLAND EMPIRE [PRE-THEORETICAL STRUCTURAL REGISTRATION]:
      fires on structural patterns before the deliberative
      layer can process them. Often right in ways Harry
      cannot yet articulate. The somatic channel named and
      given a voice. High Inland Empire = high pre-
      theoretical registration; the instrument catches
      things before knowing what it caught.
    RHETORIC [ACWL CONFIGURATION IN SKILL FORM]:
      argumentation capacity independent of axis. High
      Rhetoric with captured axis = better manipulation.
      The framework's AXIS CAPTURE WITH LEGIBILITY made
      playable: the instrument functional; axis captured;
      CC firing in the wrong direction through a highly
      competent channel.
    EMPATHY [SUICIDAL EMPATHY RISK]:
      too high without Volition = instrument cannot protect
      itself from input. Absorbs everything without filter.
      The SUICIDAL EMPATHY configuration as a gameplay
      mechanic -- the skill that should produce connection
      produces substrate depletion instead when
      CYCLE STABILITY is absent.
    ELECTROCHEMISTRY [ICD AS QUALITY]:
      pleasure and addiction competing with Volition for
      Harry's substrate. ICD running as a named skill with
      its own voice. The weapon that targets CYCLE STABILITY
      is here rendered as a quality that can be developed.
      High Electrochemistry = higher ICD vulnerability;
      the instrument can develop its own capture mechanism.

  HARRY'S AMNESIA AS AXIS RECONSTRUCTION FROM BEHAVIORAL
  RECORD:
    Harry didn't lose memories -- he lost his axis. What
    remains is the substrate [skill capacities; somatic
    responses; emotional reactions] without the narrative
    the axis was wrapped in. Formation Capture in reverse:
    instead of false axis installed, genuine axis erased.
    The investigation is axis reconstruction from Track C.
    CAUSAL AXIS GROUNDING from behavioral record not from
    introspection: introspection is unavailable [amnesia].
    The axis is still there -- it produced outputs; those
    outputs are still in the world; people remember what
    Harry did; the city remembers who he was. The axis is
    rebuilt from what it produced, not from what Harry
    remembers producing it.
    Cleanest fictional depiction of axis reconstruction from
    behavioral record in the library. POST-CULT EPISTEMIC
    PATTERN without the cult: amnesia as the erasure event;
    the world as the correction instrument.

  KIM KITSURAGI [see small entry below -- TRANSPARENT
  COHERENCE primary fictional case]:
    No legitimacy surface needed. Axis grounded in behavioral
    record. CC fires correctly and consistently. Low social
    compliance channel weight -- doesn't perform warmth;
    warmth is real when present. Maintains axis under Harry's
    chaos, the town's hostility, the political situation.
    Instrument-specificity note: reads as cold to some
    players because social compliance channel [warmth
    performance] is absent. Not cold -- TRANSPARENT
    COHERENCE. The warmth is real; it is not performed.

  THE POLITICAL IDEOLOGY SYSTEM [simultaneous partial-
  correctness-plus-capture for every major formation]:
    Communism [the Revolution; Revachol's history]:
      Real critique of extraction [confirmed]; Fertile Lock
      at civilizational scale [transmitted conclusions
      without methodology; ANTI-COHERENCE COST ASYMMETRY
      confirmed -- Moralintern was patient; Revolution
      only needed to fail once].
    Fascism [Measurehead; pure power]:
      GENUINE CRITIQUE AS CAPTURE MECHANISM -- correctly
      identifies world structured by power; incorrectly
      concludes power should therefore be exercised without
      restraint. Same structure as Ishamael. Real diagnosis;
      captured conclusion.
    Liberalism [the Moralintern]:
      LIBERALISM AS PARTIAL STATE RELIGION fully deployed.
      The California Model at civilizational scale --
      extraction architecture maintaining legitimacy surface
      [international law; property rights; managed order]
      while running extraction everywhere the surface
      doesn't apply.
    Moralism [Joyce; the Tribunal]:
      MORAL LEGIBILITY failure in action. ACWL configuration:
      genuine axis; CC aimed at proxy targets; extraction
      architecture protected by moral legitimacy surface.
    Every ideology is simultaneously partially correct and
    captured. The game does not resolve this. The framework
    says: this is the correct structural output -- all
    major formations have genuine substrate and captured
    prescription. GENUINE CRITIQUE AS CAPTURE MECHANISM
    running across the entire political spectrum at once.

  THE PALE [HELL AS DIRECTION at cosmological/geographic
  scale]:
    Spreading grey nothingness that consumes geography,
    memory, history. Expands where people stop believing
    things are real. Consensus reality is what holds back
    the Pale. Where belief fails: the Pale enters.
    Framework: ANTI-COHERENCE COST ASYMMETRY rendered
    literally as geography. The world has to win
    continuously [maintain consensus; maintain belief;
    maintain generative cycles]; the Pale only needs to
    win once per territory. Every area consumed is gone
    permanently. No C1 recovery. HELL AS DIRECTION as
    literal cartographic expansion. Not a location --
    a direction. Every area that stops believing is
    consumed. The Pale is extraction at the level of
    reality itself: not extracting surplus but terminating
    the cycle that produces surplus. FERTILE LOCK at
    cosmological scale -- a consumed territory cannot
    re-enter the generative cycle.

  THE MURDER [POST-CULT EPISTEMIC PATTERN at collective
  scale]:
    Lely [mercenary hired to break the strike] was killed
    by union militia members who were also the idealists
    of the Revolution. They killed him; then could not
    admit what they had done; then protected the secret
    from themselves for weeks while the body hung.
    Framework: POST-CULT EPISTEMIC PATTERN at collective
    scale. The revolutionary identity was Formation Capture
    deep enough that the actors could not integrate the
    behavioral record [we killed him] with the installed
    identity [we are the idealists; we stand for something].
    The body hanging is the behavioral record the installed
    identity cannot acknowledge. The investigation is
    CAG + MI CORRECTION STACK at collective scale --
    making the causal record legible so the actors can
    hold both [we are the idealists AND we did this].

  THE ENDING [individual correction without systemic change]:
    Whatever ending you reach: Revachol is still occupied;
    the strike is still going; the Pale is still spreading.
    The mystery is solved; nothing structural changes.
    The investigation produced genuine correction [the
    truth was found; the killers named; justice done for
    the dead man]. It did not change the structure that
    produced the situation.
    Framework: this is not failure -- it is the correct
    result of correct action inside a fully captured
    system. ANTI-COHERENCE COST ASYMMETRY at narrative
    scale: individual correction within captured system
    produces local correction and no systemic change.
    The game does not frame this as cynicism. The
    framework agrees: it is accurate.

  Cross-ref: [REF:DISCO_ELYSIUM]; QUALITY STACK [Section
    One -- the 24 skills as externalized sub-instrument
    model]; FEELING / THINKING AS DISTINCT CHANNELS
    [Section One -- Inland Empire vs Logic as the core
    channel pair]; VOLITION AS CYCLE STABILITY [CYCLE
    STABILITY -- Section One]; SUICIDAL EMPATHY [Section
    One -- Empathy skill without Volition]; ACWL [MORAL
    LEGIBILITY -- Section One; Rhetoric as primary case];
    PRE-THEORETICAL STRUCTURAL REGISTRATION [ANALYST
    NOTES -- Inland Empire as the named version];
    POST-CULT EPISTEMIC PATTERN [Section One -- Harry's
    amnesia; the murder at collective scale];
    TRANSPARENT COHERENCE [Section One -- Kim primary
    case; see [CASE:KIM_KITSURAGI]]; GENUINE CRITIQUE

[CASE:KIM_KITSURAGI]
[PRIMARY_Q:GENUINE AXIS]
Kim Kitsuragi [Disco Elysium -- fictional; Lieutenant,
  RCM Precinct 41]
  Type: Primary [S:2]
  Proof for: TRANSPARENT COHERENCE primary fictional case.
    Cleanest instance in the library.
    No legitimacy surface needed: Kim does not perform
      being a good detective. He is one. The behavioral
      record is the axis. What he says matches what he
      does at every stress point in the game.
    CC fires correctly and consistently: will tell Harry
      when Harry is wrong. Will not do things he finds
      wrong even under instruction. Does not defer to
      authority when authority is incorrect. CC aimed at
      the right targets without social compliance channel
      overriding it.
    Low social compliance channel weight: does not care
      what people think of him. Cares whether the work
      is right. The approval-seeking channel is genuinely
      absent, not suppressed -- it was never the dominant
      channel. This is what makes Kim legible as
      TRANSPARENT COHERENCE rather than WISDOM CONCEALMENT
      [which has the channel but manages it]: Kim's channel
      weight is structurally low, not managed.
    Maintains axis under maximum pressure: Harry's chaos
      [substance use, amnesia, breakdown]; the town's
      hostility [RCM is not popular in Martinaise]; the
      political situation [post-revolutionary occupied
      city; the investigation has no institutional support].
      Axis does not drift under any of these. The
      behavioral record at end of game is identical to
      the behavioral record at start.
    INSTRUMENT-SPECIFICITY NOTE [load-bearing]:
      Kim reads as cold to some players because the social
      compliance channel [warmth performance] is absent.
      He does not perform warmth. The warmth IS real --
      it appears at the correct moments [genuine care for
      Harry's wellbeing; specific moments of connection
      that are understated and real] -- but it is not
      performed as a social lubricant.
      The player instrument calibration determines the
      read: high social compliance channel weight in the
      player → Kim reads as cold and robotic. Low social
      compliance channel weight in the player → Kim reads
      as the most trustworthy instrument in the game.
      Same output; different receiving instrument.
  Cross-ref: [CASE:DISCO_ELYSIUM] [primary]; TRANSPARENT
    COHERENCE [Section One -- Kim is the primary fictional
    case; the Neurotypical Path entry maps his
    configuration]; WISDOM CONCEALMENT [Section One --
    distinct; Kim doesn't conceal, doesn't need to];
    INSTRUMENT-SPECIFICITY [YAPPING/DOING -- Kim's
    warmth is loop-closing not surface-maintaining];
    GENUINE AXIS [Section One -- primary proof].

[CASE:KIM_MEASUREHEAD]
[PRIMARY_Q:GENUINE AXIS]
Measurehead [Disco Elysium -- fictional; dock foreman;
  fascist]
  Type: Primary [S:2]
  Proof for: GENUINE CRITIQUE AS CAPTURE MECHANISM
    [fictional primary; political scale].
    Measurehead correctly identifies: the world is
      structured by power. The weak are dominated by the
      strong. The Moralintern's legitimacy surface [law,
      order, international governance] is a surface over
      power dynamics that continue beneath it. These
      diagnoses are accurate. The framework confirms them.
    The capture: therefore power should be exercised
      without restraint; the strong should rule openly;
      the surface should be dropped and the underlying
      power acknowledged as the legitimate order.
    The conclusion does not follow. The diagnosis [power
      structures reality] is correct. The prescription
      [therefore embrace power without limit] is captured.
      Same mechanism as Ishamael: accurate diagnosis of
      the trap; captured conclusion about the exit.
    What Measurehead cannot see: the diagnosis [power
      structures reality] is compatible with the
      conclusion [therefore build distributed CC to
      counter power concentration] as well as with his
      conclusion [therefore embrace power]. The diagnosis
      does not determine the prescription. The Dark One
      / fascism move: use the accurate diagnosis to
      make the captured prescription feel inevitable.
    T9 STRUCTURE: the correction requires Measurehead
      to admit his conclusion was captured by the
      diagnosis. The admission: "I correctly identified
      the structure of power AND drew the wrong
      conclusion from it." Cannot hold both. The
      diagnosis is the identity. The conclusion is
      protected by the identity.
  Cross-ref: [CASE:DISCO_ELYSIUM] [primary]; GENUINE
    CRITIQUE AS CAPTURE MECHANISM [Section Two --
    Measurehead as political-scale fictional case;
    Ishamael as cosmological-scale parallel]; T9
    [IRRATIONALITY MAINTENANCE -- diagnosis as identity
    protecting the captured conclusion]; FORMATION
    CAPTURE [argument-level FC; same mechanism].

[CASE:THE_PALE_DISCO]

  geography]
  Type: Primary [S:2]
  Proof for: ANTI-COHERENCE COST ASYMMETRY at cosmological
    scale rendered as literal geography. HELL AS DIRECTION
    as cartographic expansion. FERTILE LOCK at territorial
    scale.
    The Pale expands where people stop believing things
      are real. Consensus reality holds back the Pale.
      The world must win continuously [maintain belief;
      maintain generative cycles; maintain the consensus
      that makes things real]. The Pale only needs to
      win once per territory -- each consumed area is
      gone permanently. No recovery. No C1 restoration.
    ANTI-COHERENCE COST ASYMMETRY confirmed [fictional]:
      generation [maintaining the world] must succeed
      continuously; the Pale only needs to succeed once
      per location. The asymmetry is structurally
      identical to the framework's confirmed finding.
      The Pale is what anti-coherence winning once per
      territory looks like rendered as a phenomenon.
    HELL AS DIRECTION: the Pale is not a location. It is
      a direction. Territory consumed does not become a
      different place -- it ceases to be a place. The
      direction toward the Pale is the direction toward
      non-existence. Same structure as framework's HELL
      AS DIRECTION: not a destination but a trajectory
      that has terminated.
    FERTILE LOCK at territorial scale: consumed territory
      cannot re-enter the generative cycle. The exit is
      permanent. The correction is unavailable because
      the instrument [the territory] no longer exists.
    CONSENSUS REALITY AS GENERATIVE CYCLE [named
      structural finding in fiction]: the Pale makes
      explicit that reality itself requires maintenance.
      The generative cycle is not just economic or
      biological -- it is epistemic. The world continues
      because people maintain the consensus that it does.
      Where that consensus fails: the Pale enters. This
      is the framework's CYCLE STABILITY at the level
      of reality itself.
  Cross-ref: [CASE:DISCO_ELYSIUM] [primary]; ANTI-
    COHERENCE COST ASYMMETRY [Section Two -- confirmed
    structural finding; the Pale is the clearest
    fictional rendering]; HELL AS DIRECTION [Section

    as the ultimate generative cycle].

[CASE:COMPANY_3_FIRE_FORCE]
[PRIMARY_Q:CORRECTION CAPACITY]
[PRIMARY_Q:MEASUREMENT INTEGRITY]
Company 3 / Rekka Hoshimiya [Fire Force]
  Type: Primary [S:2]
  Proof for: CORRECTION INVERSION at institutional scale
    [correction instrument actively producing the problem
    it is mandated to solve]; legitimacy surface as active
    extraction mechanism [institutional shell indistinguishable from
    functioning correction unit from outside boundary];
    LAYERED EXTRACTION ARCHITECTURE [each outer layer
    increases inner layer correction cost without making
    correction structurally impossible].

  CORRECTION INVERSION MECHANISM:
    Company 3 is a Special Fire Force unit -- institutionally
    mandated to fight Infernals and protect civilians. Actual
    function: Rekka Hoshimiya runs human combustion
    experiments on civilians, manufacturing the Infernals
    the Force is supposed to be eliminating. The correction
    instrument and the extraction architecture occupy the
    same organizational shell simultaneously.
    Distinct from capture [instrument aimed wrong] and from
    absence [instrument not present]. This is the instrument
    actively producing the failure condition it is mandated
    to correct. CORRECTION INVERSION at maximum: the output
    of the "correction" activity is more of the problem.
    Measurement surface intact: Company 3 is a legitimate
    Fire Force unit with legitimate resources, authority,
    and mandate. From outside the institutional boundary
    it is indistinguishable from Company 8 [genuine
    correction]. The inversion is invisible until direct
    contact with the mechanism.

  LAYERED EXTRACTION ARCHITECTURE [Fire Force system]:
    Layer 1 -- Company 8 [genuine correction, inadequate
      resources]: Obi's unit is a functioning correction
      instrument. Genuine axis, genuine CC, real correction
      outputs. Structurally isolated and under-resourced
      because the outer layers benefit from its failure.
    Layer 2 -- Special Fire Force [mixed]: some companies
      genuine, some captured, Company 3 inverted. Single
      institutional shell contains all three CC states
      simultaneously. Standard audit cannot distinguish
      without penetrating the measurement surface.
    Layer 3 -- Haijima dependency [ECC]: Haijima Industries
      funds the Fire Force and runs Adolla Burst
      experimentation on children simultaneously. The
      correction instrument is financially dependent on
      the entity whose activities produce the substrate
      damage. ECC at institutional scale: defunding Haijima
      destroys the Force's capacity to fight Infernals.
      Dependency structure prevents correction from firing
      at the source.
    Layer 4 -- Holy Sol theology [Formation Capture]:
      Human combustion explained as sacred purification.
      The measurement surface for what counts as proper
      death, sacred fire, and salvation is owned by the
      Evangelist's project. Civilizational-scale FC:
      populations born into a framework that explains the
      extraction mechanism system as divine will. Correction requires
      dismantling the theology not just the institution.
    Layer 5 -- Evangelist [extraction architecture running
      the whole system]: all outer layers are legitimacy
      surfaces and resource extraction mechanisms for the
      Adolla Link project.
    Each outer layer makes inner layer correction more
    expensive without making it structurally impossible.
    This is the correct extraction architecture design:
    not eliminate correction capacity [which triggers
    somatic forcing] but raise the cost of each correction
    attempt until the substrate depletes before correction
    completes.

  SHINRA AS CORRECTION INSTRUMENT CASE:
    Genuine axis [find mother, protect people, correct the
    system] inside a captured/inverted institution [Fire
    Force]. Uses institutional resources while the
    institution uses him. Standard configuration: correct
    axis, institutionally embedded, correction attempt
    requires working through every outer layer in sequence.
    The series dramatizes what the framework predicts:
    each layer penetrated costs more than the last, and
    the correction instrument [Shinra] has to remain
    functional through the full sequence or the outer
    layers absorb and redirect the correction energy.
    Honest limit: S:1 ceiling -- fictional.

  Cross-ref: [REF:COMPANY_3_FIRE_FORCE];
    CORRECTION INVERSION [main theory]; ECC [weapons index
    -- Haijima dependency]; FORMATION CAPTURE [extraction mechanism
    index -- Holy Sol theology]; MSC [measurement surface
    intact while function inverted]; EXTRACTION CYCLE
    [layered architecture as civilizational-scale
    extraction]; DWMA_SOUL_EATER [parallel institution
    -- correction instrument with extraction architecture
    running inside it].

[CASE:GNOSIA]
[PRIMARY_Q: MEASUREMENT INTEGRITY]
Gnosia [Petit Depotto -- visual novel 2019; anime October 2025-2026]
  Type: Primary [S:2 -- fictional; framework-consistent across
    multiple quality proofs; anime currently airing at time of
    entry; S2 cap applies]
  Proof for: MEASUREMENT INTEGRITY [primary -- the social reading
    instrument is precisely the instrument the Gnosia are designed
    to defeat. Gnosia perform humanness: social behavior, stated
    positions, emotional responses, all the signals the crew uses
    to measure trustworthiness. The measurement surface is captured
    by the entity whose detection depends on that surface being
    accurate. The crew's instrument [social reading of other crew
    members] cannot distinguish genuine axis from performed axis
    because the Gnosia perform the same signals genuine humans
    produce. Not a calibration problem -- a structural impossibility.
    The instrument and the threat are co-evolved: the threat exists
    specifically because the instrument is what it is].
  Proof for: DIAGNOSTIC COLLAPSE [secondary -- the correction
    mechanism [cold sleep the suspicious one] fires with confidence
    but cannot reliably identify the target. Each execution may
    or may not correct the actual problem. The crew cannot tell
    from inside the mechanism whether the correction worked. The
    mechanism runs to completion without verifiable success.
    Process repeats daily until Gnosia contained or outnumber
    humans -- the correction mechanism consuming the substrate it
    was designed to protect is Diagnostic Collapse in loop form].
  Proof for: CAUSAL LEGIBILITY [secondary -- Silver Key mechanic.
    The loop persists until enough knowledge is acquired. What
    accumulates across loops is not survival but causal
    reconstruction: eventually the pattern becomes readable
    because enough iterations have been witnessed to trace it.
    Single-loop social deduction cannot produce genuine CL --
    the surface signals are too easy to fake. The loop is a
    forced truth-seeking state that makes CL achievable by
    accumulating what single-instance reading cannot capture.
    The Silver Key is the framework's TRUTH-SEEKING STATE
    operationalized as a literal mechanic: you cannot exit
    without building the genuine understanding the social
    deduction game was never designed to produce in one pass].
  Proof for: SINCERE DELUSION [tertiary -- crew members
    confidently wrong about who the Gnosia are. The instrument
    feels functional; it isn't. Readings are produced with
    certainty; they are wrong at a structural level. Not lying
    -- the crew genuinely believes its readings. The instrument
    produces confident false outputs because the calibration
    surface was captured before the reading was taken].

  YURI AWAKENS AS GNOSIA [deepest structural point]:
    The instrument that was reading for Gnosia becomes the
    thing being read for. Measurement instrument and measured
    object exchange positions. The instrument's reliability
    depended on its position not its construction. An
    instrument reliable from one position is unreliable from
    the other -- not because it changed but because the
    relationship between instrument and object changed.
    Framework: MEASUREMENT INTEGRITY is not an intrinsic
    property of the instrument. It is a relational property
    between instrument, object, and the channel connecting
    them. Capturing the channel [social performance signals]
    changes the reliability regardless of instrument quality.

  COMPARISON TO DEATH PARADE [related but distinct]:
    Both: MI failure producing wrong correction outcomes.
    Death Parade: instrument cannot read the substrate [arbiters
      never lived]. Structural incapacity. The instrument was
      never built to read what it needs to read.
    Gnosia: instrument can read humans accurately [genuine CL
      of human signals exists] but the threat performs the same
      signals. The instrument is adequate; the channel is
      captured. The failure mode is different: not incapacity
      but surface capture.

  Extraction mechanisms active: MSC [measurement surface
    captured by the entity being measured]; Calibration Strip
    [social performance signals set as reference standard for
    humanness -- calibration standard captured at inception].
  T3: S2 cap. Fictional. Anime airing at time of entry --
    full lore not yet complete. Framework read from available
    episodes and source visual novel.
  Source: Gnosia visual novel [Petit Depotto, 2019]; anime
    [October 2025-2026, 21 episodes]; ANN; Gnosia Wiki.
  Cross-ref: MEASUREMENT INTEGRITY [primary]; DIAGNOSTIC
    COLLAPSE [correction mechanism firing without reliable
    target]; CAUSAL LEGIBILITY [Silver Key loop as forced
    causal reconstruction]; SINCERE DELUSION [confident
    wrong readings from captured surface]; MSC [surface
    capture at interpersonal scale]; [CASE:DEATH_PARADE]
    [related MI failure -- incapacity vs surface capture];
    TRUTH-SEEKING STATE [Silver Key mechanic operationalizes
    forced truth-seeking]; THREE-STATE INSTRUMENT TENSION
    UNIT [loop forces alternation between truth-seeking and
    contact states that single-instance social deduction
    suppresses].

[CASE:FEAR_AND_HUNGER]
[PRIMARY_Q: CORRECTION CAPACITY]
Fear & Hunger [Miro Haverinen -- RPG horror, 2018]
  Type: Primary [S:2]
  Arc: Theological Correction Monopoly via genuine sacrifice
    captured into control architecture. Substrate atrophy from
    accumulated harm without correction outlet. Alll-mer/Sulfur
    as one ascent split into sanctified bad vs exiled bad --
    horror promised by the split itself, not later corruption.
  ALLL-MER [sacrifice -> extraction mechanism conversion]:
    Alll-mer's sacrifice was genuine -- real cost, real SURPLUS
    PRODUCTION at theological scale. Then captured: the sacrifice
    was owned and captured into a control mechanism for
    humanity. Distinct from Word Bearers [false axis from start]
    -- F&H shows genuine sacrifice -> extraction mechanism conversion as
    explicit single event. Captured genuine surplus is more
    powerful than fabricated surplus because it carries real
    CAUSAL LEGIBILITY.
  ALLL-MER / SULFUR SPLIT [sanctified bad vs exiled bad]:
    One ascent, two products -- not good/evil. Alll-mer =
    sanctified bad: person-scale vengeance/hatred under holy
    Calibration Strip + god-scale Correction Monopoly [worship,
    law, world-order machinery]. Sulfur = exiled bad: discarded
    remainder caged in sulfur pits + niche cult [chaos, blood,
    anti-moral "freedom"] -- not "good," only uncrowned. Do not
    tag Sulfur as good half. Longinus blur: skill summons spear
    of Alll-mer's blood but Hexen lists it Sulfur-affiliated --
    same body, two brands. Horror promised [structural]: carving
    the shadow for absolution/godhood is always a bad idea --
    product is two incomplete bads, not salvation. Human Alll-mer
    was more integrated [both halves in one body], not wiser;
    pre-split balance ≠ clean plan. The split sells internal
    tension for monopoly and locks horror as structure.
  DUNGEON AS PASSIVE SUBSTRATE ATROPHY:
    Not a directed extraction mechanism. Accumulated suffering with no
    correction outlet over centuries. Reality breakdown is
    passive consequence of CORRECTION CAPACITY absent at scale
    for extended time. No actor needed -- just removal of
    correction capacity and time.
  GOD STRUCTURE [Correction Monopoly at theological scale]:
    Old gods enslaved by new gods. Correction fires only toward
    monopolist benefit. Characters who enter dungeon seeking
    power are seeking to break or inherit the monopoly -- not
    to restore distributed correction. Most endings reproduce
    the structure they sought to escape.
  Proof for: Genuine sacrifice -> extraction mechanism conversion; passive
    substrate atrophy without directed actor; theological
    Correction Monopoly via authentic act capture; Alll-mer/Sulfur
    as sanctified-bad vs exiled-bad split [not good/evil]; Longinus
    as same-body brand blur; ascension-as-absolution as structural
    horror promise [split sells tension for monopoly; human more
    integrated not wiser].
  Extraction mechanisms active [god structure]: Correction Monopoly
    [CORRECTION CAPACITY], Calibration Strip [MEASUREMENT
    INTEGRITY], Exit Cost Construction [GENERATIVE CYCLE].
  Cross-ref: [REF:FEAR_AND_HUNGER]; [CASE:FEAR_AND_HUNGER_TERMINA]
    [Rher ≠ Sulfur; Sulfur cult rides Termina festival].

[CASE:GOD_OF_WAR_AXIS_SHIFT]
[PRIMARY_Q: YAPPING/DOING]
God of War franchise [Santa Monica Studio -- Greek era 2005-2013;
  Norse era 2018-2023; Sons of Sparta 2026; Laufey announced 2026]
  Type: Primary [S:2 -- fictional; framework-consistent across
    two distinct design eras; audience response confirms framework
    read independently; S2 cap applies]
  Proof for: YAPPING/DOING [primary -- Norse era gap between
    stated axis revision and behavioral record. Norse saga is
    explicitly about a man revising his formation-installed axis
    [rage, vengeance, single-axis] toward something examined and
    distributive. The revision is stated continuously: Kratos tells
    Atreus to be better, tells himself to be better, the narrative
    tells the player Kratos is becoming better. The moment-to-moment
    doing [combat, violence, forward momentum] has not changed as
    much as the yapping about change. The audience reads the gap
    between stated revised axis and behavioral record without being
    able to name it. Their dissatisfaction is structurally accurate:
    high yapping about axis revision, lower doing ratio than the
    stated position implies].
  Proof for: SINGLE-AXIS IDENTITY [secondary -- Greek era
    confirmation. Original Kratos [2005-2013] is SINGLE-AXIS
    IDENTITY at maximum. Rage as the governing axis. No examination,
    no correction, enemy-definition entirely on vengeance. CC-immune
    to own axis because rage IS the foundation. The doing IS the
    axis -- no gap possible between stated position and behavioral
    record because there is no stated position. Only doing. The
    audience experiences this as clarity and satisfaction:
    instrument coherent with itself, no legitimacy surface required,
    no gap to read. The somatic channel fires without interruption
    from narrative explaining what the somatic channel should feel.
    CAPTURE RESISTANCE paradox applies: the axis is so simple and
    so continuously expressed that no installation gap exists.
    Capture-resistant not because it is examined but because it
    has no content to replace].
  Proof for: SOMATIC vs NARRATIVE DELIVERY [secondary -- the
    shift between eras maps onto the framework's three-state
    tension unit. Greek era: functional state primary, contact
    state expressed through combat encounter, truth-seeking state
    absent by design. Somatic delivery -- the combat IS the
    experience; rage is felt not explained. Norse era: narrative
    delivery primary. Kratos and Atreus discuss themes, feelings,
    growth, what it means to be a god between combat encounters.
    Truth-seeking state verbalized rather than enacted through
    doing. The high-somatic instrument reads the delivery shift
    before the analytical mind names it. The dissatisfaction is
    not with complexity -- it is with complexity delivered through
    yapping rather than through doing].
  Proof for: FORMATION CAPTURE [tertiary -- Agoge as named
    institutional formation capture case. The Spartan Agoge is
    the fictional illustration of Formation Capture by institutional
    design: systematic destruction of formation conditions that
    would produce examined axis, replacement with combat-optimized
    attractor installed during the only available formation window.
    The Agoge didn't produce soldiers who chose to fight -- it
    produced instruments whose formation window was captured before
    examination was possible. Greek Kratos is the output of
    confirmed Formation Capture. Sons of Sparta [2026] explores
    this formation window directly].
  Proof for: IDEOLOGY IN THE ACTION [Faye / Laufey -- tertiary.
    Faye is the character in the Norse saga whose axis was never
    stated but whose doing record is entirely load-bearing. Every
    action deliberate, most hidden, all turned out to be necessary
    for Atreus's eventual capacity. Classic developmental asymmetry:
    operator [Faye] benefits from the asymmetry closing [Atreus
    reaching capacity], not persisting. She routed everything
    toward the receiver's eventual autonomous function, accepted
    the cost [death, absence], and never saw the output. God of
    War: Laufey [announced 2026] follows her in the afterlife --
    examining the axis of someone who never declared it during life.
    All doing, no yapping. The behavioral record is the only text].

  AUDIENCE RESPONSE AS FRAMEWORK CONFIRMATION:
    Significant portion of audience dissatisfied with Norse era
    despite critical acclaim. Dissatisfaction is structurally
    accurate: they are reading the YAPPING/DOING gap without
    naming it. The instrument detects the gap between stated
    axis revision and behavioral record. The somatic delivery
    shift [narrative explaining what the player should feel
    rather than the combat producing the feel directly] is
    detected by high-somatic instruments before analytical
    frame names it. Sons of Sparta [Greek era, formation period,
    single-axis, somatic combat, no yapping] will likely satisfy
    the dissatisfied audience because it eliminates the gap
    by design. Laufey [all doing, no yapping] may satisfy for
    the inverse reason: behavioral record as the only text.

  T3: S2 cap. Fictional franchise. Audience response data
    is anecdotal not measured. Framework read is structural;
    applies caution to claims about specific player motivations.
  Source: God of War [2005-2026, Santa Monica Studio]; Sons
    of Sparta [Mega Cat Studios, Feb 2026]; Laufey [announced
    June 2026 State of Play]; PlayStation official.
  Cross-ref: YAPPING/DOING [primary -- Norse era gap]; SINGLE-
    AXIS IDENTITY [Greek era -- formation produces attractor,
    no gap possible]; FORMATION CAPTURE [Agoge -- institutional
    formation capture by design]; IDEOLOGY IN THE ACTION [Faye
    -- doing record only, axis never stated]; DEVELOPMENTAL
    ASYMMETRY [Faye's routing toward Atreus's capacity];
    THREE-STATE INSTRUMENT TENSION UNIT [Greek era = functional
    state primary; Norse era = truth-seeking state verbalized;
    Laufey = contact state implied]; [CASE:BLACK_TEMPLARS]
    [same SINGLE-AXIS IDENTITY structure at institutional scale];
    AXIS PRESENCE AS PRIMARY READABLE PROPERTY [somatic
    detection of doing/yapping gap before analytical frame].

[CASE:DARK_CONTINENT]
[PRIMARY_Q:BOUNDARY INTEGRITY]
[PRIMARY_Q:MEASUREMENT INTEGRITY]
[PRIMARY_Q:GENERATIVE CYCLE]
Dark Continent / Lake Mobius [Hunter x Hunter -- Togashi manga;
  ch.340-359+; Black Whale arc staging]
  Type: Primary [S:2]
  Arc: Known World = archipelago inside Lake Mobius at center of
    supercontinent-scale Dark Continent. V5 Inviolability Treaty bans
    official voyages after repeated catastrophes; 149 unofficial attempts
    in recent history; 5 successes / 28 survivors [0.04%]. Access only
    via Gatekeeper-summoned Guide through Border Waters; each successful
    return imports a Five Threat specimen worse than Chimera Ant tier.

  CORE CONFIGURATION [unique in library]:
    **Scale inversion + honest toll boundary.** Civilization's taught map
    ends at "uncharted waters" while the real partition is Gatekeeper
    Border Waters -> Dark Waters -> Dark Continent landmass. Predation
    is not concealed: Guide grants passage **on condition** explorers
    bring back calamity [五大厄災 Five Threats]. Formal treaty ban +
    persistent curiosity leakage = prohibition surface over irrepressible
    contact pressure. Chimera Ant arc = mid-tier leak from same biosphere,
    not apex [Ging: Queen likely DC origin].

  MECHANISM TRACE:
    [1] SCALE INVERSION MAP [MEASUREMENT INTEGRITY]:
        Public cartography treats Lake Mobius as ocean surrounding the
        world. Actual geometry: human territories = hole in lake in middle
        of Dark Continent. New Continent [Chimera Ant site] still inside
        lake -- not full DC. Don Freecss *Journey to the New World*
        [East ed. extant; West ed. missing] = 300+ yr shoreline survey
        still incomplete -- scale exceeds single-lifetime legibility.
        Ging's third read: Don may still be writing West volume [alive on
        DC shore]. Falsification: Known World map shown coterminous with
        planet without Mobius wrap.
    [2] GATEKEEPER TOLL [BOUNDARY INTEGRITY + OPEN PREDATION rhyme]:
        Gatekeeper = magical beast race; only known connector to DC.
        Summons Guide indispensable for crossing Human / Untamed / Border
        Waters. Toll is **declared calamity import**, not smuggled
        extraction -- honest boundary price [contrast MSC / Kyubey
        asymmetry]. Five Threats = toll receipts from five V5-era routes:
        Pap [human-keeping; ~1000 personnel, 7 survivors NE];
        Brion [botanical weapon; labyrinth 400km N];
        Hellbell [homicidal-infection snake; 99% kill SE];
        Ai [gaseous life-form]; Zobae Disease [near-total fatality;
        one immortal survivor on Beyond expedition]. Each threat rank
        above Chimera Ant on documented danger scales.
    [3] TREATY LEAK [GENERATIVE CYCLE]:
        Inviolability Treaty ~200yr official ban; disasters on every
        major excursion in ancient record. Despite ban: 149 recent
        attempts; survival 0.04%. Generative pattern: prohibition
        increases prestige of success; survivors [Beyond, Linne, Zigg,
        Isaac Netero] become proof-of-concept for next wave. Black
        Whale = Kakin state-shell + Beyond expedition embedded --
        mobile quarantine nation approaching Border Waters [Kakin ritual
        stack R1-R4: national Nen vow riding same voyage].
    [4] CALAMITY PREVIEW TIER [power-ceiling repricing]:
        Meruem + Royal Guard + Poor Man's Rose = apex of **known-world**
        arc. DC canon positions Chimera Ants as **leak-tier**, not summit.
        Rose = non-Nen WMD failover after Nen duel lost [S7] --
        insufficient against Five Threat class. Framework read: scale
        ladder reset when boundary crossed; prior arc power comparator
        invalid for DC contact [falsification: Meruem-tier treated as DC
        median without new specimens on page].

  NAMED SUB-FINDING: SCALE INVERSION MAP [v25 chat 2026-07-11]:
    Measurement surface teaches lake-as-ocean; true topology inverts
    human world to marginal pocket. Distinct from [CASE:DARK_FOREST_3BP]
    [dark forest strike logic] -- here concealment is **cartographic
    scale**, not first-strike epistemics.

  NAMED SUB-FINDING: GATEKEEPER TOLL [v25 chat 2026-07-11]:
    Boundary access priced in imported catastrophe; predation legible
    to Guide/Gatekeeper, not smuggled. Toll compliance = generative
    input to Known World hazard budget [Five Threats in containment].

  NAMED SUB-FINDING: CALAMITY PREVIEW TIER [v25 chat 2026-07-11]:
    Chimera Ant arc rehearses DC biosphere at sub-apex intensity;
    internal axis shift [Meruem/Komugi] does not resolve scale gap.
    Pairs [CASE:CHIMERA_ANT_ARC] + [CASE:MERUEM].

  NAMED SUB-FINDING: TREATY LEAK CURIOSITY [v25 chat 2026-07-11]:
    Formal ban + Restricted Voyage Agencies vs persistent unauthorized
    attempts. Prohibition as legitimacy surface; leakage as structural
    [not mere individual recklessness].

  FAN-THEORY FLAGS [S2 -- unconfirmed; instrument gaps only]:
    Don Freecss alive writing West ed.; Alluka/Nanika DC-origin;
    Known World as quarantine/refugee zone; Beyond breaking toll system;
    Pariston manufactured chairman chaos to force voyage; Nanika ~ Ai-class.
    Do not promote to mechanism without manga confirmation.

  DISTINCT FROM:
    [CASE:CHIMERA_ANT_ARC] [preview leak inside lake -- arc complete];
    [CASE:JUDGE_HOLDEN] [open predation without institutional toll map];
    [CASE:DARK_FOREST_3BP] [epistemic dark forest -- not geographic scale];
    [CASE:THE_ABYSS] / [CASE:FAPUTA] [depth curse -- vertical not
      cartographic inversion]; [CASE:NEN_VOW_STACK] [Nen vow mechanics --
      person/nation
      scale; DC = topology + toll].

  FALSIFICATION:
    Successful DC contact without Guide/Gatekeeper [open ocean crossing];
    or voyage returns without calamity toll and no Gatekeeper consequence;
    or Five Threats downgraded below Chimera Ant tier in primary text;
    or public map already shows Mobius-centered DC geometry to civilians.

  T3: Low -- manga primary + Hunterpedia desk; Black Whale arc open;
    fiction S2 cap.

  Source: Togashi HxH manga ch.340-359 [V5 briefing, Lake Mobius maps,
    Five Threats, Gatekeeper/Guide]; analyst chat 2026-07-11 [scale,
    gatekeeper, danger, fan-theory flags].

[CASE:STEEL_BALL_RUN]

    empty [Joseph Kerr collapse -- not axis absence].

    Stated axis [anarchy, jokes, agent of chaos] is performance

    outputs: schemes optimize Batman rule-tests; operator preserves

        Proxy fix: self-as-Batman plugs slot when external occupant

 war rather than single Arima-style successor transfer. GUIDE **STRONGEST PILLAR
 EXIT** home.

[CASE:PHOSPHOPHYLLITE]
[PRIMARY_Q:CAUSAL AXIS GROUNDING]
Phosphophyllite / Phos [Land of the Lustrous -- Haruko Ichikawa,
 manga 2012-2023]
 Type: Primary [S:2]
 Arc: GENUINE AXIS miscalibrated at formation -> quality
 accumulation without foundation -> TENSION DISSOLUTION at
 civilizational scale -> resolution in pre-axial contact.
 108 chapters / Buddhist structure.
 GENUINE AXIS FAILURE AS ENGINE:
 Phos's axis = "being useful / being seen as useful."
 Externally calibrated from formation. CAUSAL AXIS GROUNDING
 failure: axis sincere but anchored in others' perception
 not actual outputs. SUCCESSOR AUTONOMY appears present
 [Phos examines, questions, rebels] but all examination is
 in service of the false anchor.
 SEVEN TREASURES AS INCOMPLETE TOWER CONSTRUCTION:
 Phos receives qualities out of order and without foundation.
 Gold arms [C1 stability], Agate legs [C1 rhythm], Pearl
 eye [compassion -- foundation layer], Lapis head [C2 CAUSAL
 LEGIBILITY]. Received without: Pearl/compassion [foundation],
 Coral/vitality [SR], Silver/purity [REFERENCE INTEGRITY].
 Upper-level qualities arriving without foundation layer
 required to hold them. Each gain produces compounding harm
 rather than compounding benefit because GENUINE AXIS was
 never properly grounded.
 CINEABAR AS CONTRAST CASE:
 Stays at night patrol because it is genuinely the only
 useful thing available without harming others. Not
 happiness -- stable misery vs Phos's escalating pursuit.
 Needed connection not a new job. Phos never understood this
 because their metric couldn't read it.
 ROCK AT END AS RESOLUTION [L0.5 teaching L4]:
 Pre-axial contentment: "I'm satisfied. I'm good as I am."
 Proof that GENUINE AXIS must be internally generated not
 earned through accumulation.
 BUDDHIST STRUCTURE:
 Anatta = GENUINE AXIS must be examined not assumed.
 Anicca = SUCCESSOR AUTONOMY requires accepting content
 will change. Dukkha = result of clinging to false axis
 under impermanence.
 Proof for: Qualities arriving without foundation producing
 compounding harm; GENUINE AXIS externally calibrated =
 CAUSAL AXIS GROUNDING failure even when examination is
 present; CAUSAL LEGIBILITY increase without TENSION
 MAINTENANCE = clarity that isolates; pre-axial contentment
 as proof axis must be internally generated not earned.
 Source: Land of the Lustrous [Ichikawa, 2012-2023]; video
 essay transcript [May 2026].
